			U390.Purchase = function()

		local function F_000956(A956_1)
			if A956_1.X <= A956_1.Y and A956_1.X <= A956_1.Z then

		local function F_000957(A957_1)

		end

		U645.TryHit = function(A969_1, A969_2)
			F_000965()
			if workspace:GetAttribute("PvPDisabled") == true then
				return "Player hits are off right now"
			end
			local L969_1 = U8.Character
			local L969_2 = L969_1 and L969_1:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local L969_3 = U645.Humanoid(L969_1)
			if not L969_2 or not L969_3 or L969_3.Health <= 0 then
				return "Waiting for your character"
			end
			local L969_4 = U645.PickBat(L969_1)
			if not L969_4 then
				return "No bat found"
			end

			if not F_000950(L969_1, L969_3, L969_4) then
				return "Equipping " .. tostring(L969_4:GetAttribute("GearName") or L969_4.Name)
			end

			if not U645.Hittable(A969_1) or U645.Ragdolled(A969_1) then
				return nil
			end
			A969_2 = A969_2 or U645.Plan(A969_1, L969_2)
			if not A969_2 then
				return nil
			end
			local L969_5 = U645.Range(L969_4) - U647
			local L969_6 = L969_2.Position - L969_2.AssemblyLinearVelocity * U648
			if (A969_2.Historical - L969_6).Magnitude > L969_5 and (A969_2.Current - L969_6).Magnitude > L969_5 then
				return nil
			end
			local U663 = F_000940()
			if not U663 then
				return nil
			end
			local L969_7 = math.clamp(U8:GetNetworkPing(), 0, 1)
			local L969_8 = tonumber(L969_4:GetAttribute("CooldownEndTime")) or 0
			if F_000939() < L969_8 - L969_7 * 0.5 then
				return nil
			end
			local L969_9 = U655.LastFire
			if os.clock() - L969_9 < math.max(0.12, L969_7 * 1.5) then
				return nil
			end
			U655.LastFire = os.clock()
			U655.Trace = U655.Trace + 1

			table.insert(U655.Pending, {
				Target = A969_1,
				Option = A969_2.Option,
				At = os.clock(),
				Wait = math.max(0.5, L969_7 * 2 + 0.3),
				RagdollBefore = F_000941(A969_1),
				CooldownBefore = L969_8,
				Tool = L969_4,
			})

			local U664 = string.format("%d:%d:%d", U8.UserId, U655.Trace, math.floor(F_000939() * 1000))

			pcall(function()
				U663:FireServer(A969_1, U664)
			end)

			return "Hitting " .. A969_1.DisplayName
		end

					local L974_2, L974_3, L974_4 = pcall(function()
						return I525.Part0, I525.Part1
					end)

							if typeof(I527) == "Instance" and not I527:IsDescendantOf(L974_1) then

								if L974_5 then

								end

								L974_5 = L974_5 or nil
								if L974_5 and L974_5 ~= U8 and L974_5:IsA("Player") then
									return L974_5
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		U678.FormatRate = function(A1237_1)
			local L1237_1 = tonumber(A1237_1) or 0
			if L1237_1 >= 1e12 then
				return string.format("%.2fT/s", L1237_1 / 1e12)
			end

			if L1237_1 >= 1e9 then
				return string.format("%.2fB/s", L1237_1 / 1e9)
			end

			if L1237_1 >= 1000000 then
				return string.format("%.2fM/s", L1237_1 / 1000000)
			end

			if L1237_1 >= 1000 then
				return string.format("%.1fK/s", L1237_1 / 1000)
			end
			return string.format("%d/s", math.floor(L1237_1))
		end

		U678.FormatWeight = function(A1238_1)
			local L1238_1 = tonumber(A1238_1) or 0
			local L1238_2 = L1238_1 >= 1000 and string.format("%.0f", L1238_1) or string.format("%.2f", L1238_1)
			local L1238_3, L1238_4 = string.match(L1238_2, "^(%-?%d+)(%.%d+)$")
			L1238_3 = L1238_3 or L1238_2
			local L1238_5

			while true do
				local L1238_6
				L1238_5, L1238_6 = string.gsub(L1238_3, "^(%-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")

				if L1238_6 ~= 0 then
					L1238_3 = L1238_5
				else
					break
				end
			end

			return L1238_5 .. (L1238_4 or "") .. " Kg"
		end
